In Autonomous Driving (AD), this synergy has enabled Vision Language Action (VLA) models, which translate high-level multimodal understanding into driving behaviors, typically represented as future trajectories. However, existing VLA models mainly generate generic collision-free trajectories. Beyond collision avoidance, adapting to diverse driving styles (e.g., sporty, comfortable) is essential for personalized driving. Moreover, many methods treat trajectory generation as naive token prediction, which can produce kinematically infeasible actions. To address these limitations, we present StyleVLA, a physics-informed VLA framework for generating diverse and physically plausible driving behaviors. We introduce a hybrid loss that combines a kinematic consistency constraint with a continuous regression head to improve trajectory feasibility. To train StyleVLA, built on Qwen3-VL-4B, we construct a large-scale instruction dataset with over 1.2k scenarios, 76k Bird's Eye View (BEV) samples, and 42k First Person View (FPV) samples, with ground-truth trajectories for five driving styles and natural-language instructions. Experiments show that our 4B-parameter StyleVLA significantly outperforms proprietary models (e.g., Gemini-3-Pro) and state-of-the-art VLA models. Using a composite driving score measuring success rate, physical feasibility, and style adherence, StyleVLA achieves 0.55 on BEV and 0.51 on FPV, versus 0.32 and 0.35 for Gemini-3-Pro. These results show that a specialized, physics-informed, lightweight model can surpass closed-source models on domain-specific tasks.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Short-Horizon: VQ-Memory for Robust Long-Horizon Manipulation in Non-Markovian Simulation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09513)
*Wang Honghui,Jing Zhi,Ao Jicong,Song Shiji,Li Xuelong,Huang Gao,Bai Chenjia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RuleSafe是一个基于LLM辅助仿真框架的铰接式操作基准，包含多种解锁机制的安全箱任务，提出VQ-Memory作为紧凑结构化时序表示来提升长时程规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人仿真基准主要关注简单的拾取放置任务，无法捕捉现实世界任务的非马尔可夫特性和铰接物体交互的复杂性，且真实机器人数据收集成本高昂。

Method: 1) 构建RuleSafe基准，包含钥匙锁、密码锁、逻辑锁等多种解锁机制的安全箱任务，通过LLM生成规则创建非马尔可夫和长时程任务；2) 提出VQ-Memory表示，使用向量量化变分自编码器(VQ-VAE)将过去本体感知状态编码为离散潜在标记，过滤低级噪声同时保留高级任务阶段上下文。

Result: 在先进VLA模型和扩散策略上的大量实验表明，VQ-Memory能持续改进长时程规划，增强对未见配置的泛化能力，并以更低的计算成本实现更高效的操作。

Conclusion: RuleSafe基准和VQ-Memory表示共同解决了现有仿真基准的局限性，为铰接式操作提供了更真实的评估平台，同时VQ-Memory的紧凑结构化时序表示能有效提升机器人策略在复杂长时程任务中的性能。

Abstract: The high cost of collecting real-robot data has made robotic simulation a scalable platform for both evaluation and data generation. Yet most existing benchmarks concentrate on simple manipulation tasks such as pick-and-place, failing to capture the non-Markovian characteristics of real-world tasks and the complexity of articulated object interactions. To address this limitation, we present RuleSafe, a new articulated manipulation benchmark built upon a scalable LLM-aided simulation framework. RuleSafe features safes with diverse unlocking mechanisms, such as key locks, password locks, and logic locks, which require different multi-stage reasoning and manipulation strategies. These LLM-generated rules produce non-Markovian and long-horizon tasks that require temporal modeling and memory-based reasoning. We further propose VQ-Memory, a compact and structured temporal representation that uses vector-quantized variational autoencoders (VQ-VAEs) to encode past proprioceptive states into discrete latent tokens. This representation filters low-level noise while preserving high-level task-phase context, providing lightweight yet robust temporal cues that are compatible with existing Vision-Language-Action models (VLA). Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art VLA models and diffusion policies show that VQ-Memory consistently improves long-horizon planning, enhances generalization to unseen configurations, and enables more efficient manipulation with reduced computational cost. Project page: vqmemory.github.io

</details>


### [190] [NS-VLA: Towards Neuro-Symbolic Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09542)
*Ziyue Zhu,Shangyang Wu,Shuai Zhao,Zhiqiu Zhao,Shengjie Li,Yi Wang,Fang Li,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NS-VLA框架，通过在线强化学习解决VLA模型在可重用基元学习、数据依赖和探索能力方面的挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型面临三个主要挑战：1) 难以学习相关且可重用的基元；2) 过度依赖大规模数据和复杂架构；3) 缺乏超越演示的探索能力。需要一种更高效、更具泛化性的解决方案。

Method: 提出神经符号VLA框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 符号编码器将视觉和语言特征嵌入并提取结构化基元；2) 符号求解器实现数据高效的动作序列生成；3) 在线强化学习优化生成过程并扩展探索空间。

Result: 在机器人操作基准测试中，NS-VLA在单次训练和数据扰动设置下均优于先前方法，同时展现出：1) 优越的零样本泛化能力；2) 高数据效率；3) 扩展的探索空间。

Conclusion: NS-VLA框架通过神经符号结合和在线强化学习，有效解决了VLA模型的关键挑战，在性能、效率和探索能力方面取得显著改进，为机器人操作任务提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are formulated to ground instructions in visual context and generate action sequences for robotic manipulation. Despite recent progress, VLA models still face challenges in learning related and reusable primitives, reducing reliance on large-scale data and complex architectures, and enabling exploration beyond demonstrations. To address these challenges, we propose a novel Neuro-Symbolic Vision-Language-Action (NS-VLA) framework via online reinforcement learning (RL). It introduces a symbolic encoder to embedding vision and language features and extract structured primitives, utilizes a symbolic solver for data-efficient action sequencing, and leverages online RL to optimize generation via expansive exploration. Experiments on robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that NS-VLA outperforms previous methods in both one-shot training and data-perturbed settings, while simultaneously exhibiting superior zero-shot generalizability, high data efficiency and expanded exploration space. Our code is available.

</details>


### [191] [Trajectory Optimization for Self-Wrap-Aware Cable-Towed Planar Object Manipulation under Implicit Tension Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09557)
*Yu Li,Amin Fakhari,Hamid Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种考虑自缠绕的缆绳牵引操纵轨迹优化方法，通过路由感知、张力隐式的优化框架耦合张力约束和路由条件传输映射，并构建了从严格模式到三种可处理松弛的层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆绳/绳索在可变形物体操纵中普遍存在，作为力传递介质，其路由和接触决定了力矩传递方式。在缆绳牵引操纵中，传递是单向和混合的：缆绳仅在拉紧时产生拉力，松弛时无力；实践中缆绳可能接触物体边界并自缠绕边缘，这不仅涉及碰撞避免，更改变了力矩传递通道，通过改变有效作用点和力臂，将路由几何与刚体运动和张力耦合。

Method: 将自缠绕牵引建模为路由感知、张力隐式的轨迹优化问题，耦合：(i) 张力隐式的拉紧/松弛约束；(ii) 路由条件的有效长度和力矩传输映射。构建了从严格模式条件参考到三种可处理松弛的层次结构：全模式松弛、二元模式松弛和隐式模式松弛。

Result: 在平面牵引任务中，将路由作为显式决策通常产生保守解，保持在切换边界附近；而隐式模式松弛通过状态演化诱导自缠绕，并在需要转向时利用重定向的扭矩通道。

Conclusion: 自缠绕牵引的优化方法能够有效处理缆绳路由与刚体运动的耦合，隐式模式松弛策略相比显式路由决策更能利用自缠绕带来的力矩传递优势，在转向等任务中表现更优。

Abstract: Cable/rope elements are pervasive in deformable-object manipulation, often serving as a deformable force-transmission medium whose routing and contact determine how wrenches are delivered. In cable-towed manipulation, transmission is unilateral and hybrid: the tether can pull only when taut and becomes force-free when slack; in practice, the tether may also contact the object boundary and self-wrap around edges, which is not merely collision avoidance but a change of the wrench transmission channel by shifting the effective application point and moment arm, thereby coupling routing geometry with rigid-body motion and tensioning. We formulate self-wrap towing as a routing-aware, tensioning-implicit trajectory optimization (TITO) problem that couples (i) a tensioning-implicit taut/slack constraint and (ii) routing-conditioned transmission maps for effective length and wrench, and we build a relaxation hierarchy from a strict mode-conditioned reference to three tractable relaxations: Full-Mode Relaxation (FMR), Binary-Mode Relaxation (BMR), and Implicit-Mode Relaxation (IMR). Across planar towing tasks, we find that making routing an explicit decision often yields conservative solutions that stay near switching boundaries, whereas IMR induces self-wrap through state evolution and exploits the redirected torque channel whenever turning requires it.

</details>


### [192] [SCDP: Learning Humanoid Locomotion from Partial Observations via Mixed-Observation Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09574)
*Milo Carroll,Tianhu Peng,Lingfan Bao,Chengxu Zhou,Zhibin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCDP是一种仅使用机载传感器实现人形机器人运动控制的方法，无需显式状态估计，通过传感器条件扩散策略和混合观测训练实现部署策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖需要复杂且不可靠状态估计的特权全身状态，从离线数据集提取可部署的人形运动控制策略仍具挑战性

Method: 传感器条件扩散策略(SCDP)通过混合观测训练解耦感知与监督：扩散模型以传感器历史为条件，同时被监督预测特权未来状态-动作轨迹；开发了受限去噪、上下文分布对齐和上下文感知注意力掩码

Result: 在仿真中，速度控制成功率99-100%，AMASS测试集跟踪成功率93%，性能与特权基线相当；在真实G1人形机器人上以50Hz部署，无需外部传感或状态估计

Conclusion: SCDP成功实现了仅使用机载传感器的人形机器人运动控制，消除了对显式状态估计的依赖，在仿真和真实机器人上都展示了鲁棒性能

Abstract: Distilling humanoid locomotion control from offline datasets into deployable policies remains a challenge, as existing methods rely on privileged full-body states that require complex and often unreliable state estimation. We present Sensor-Conditioned Diffusion Policies (SCDP) that enables humanoid locomotion using only onboard sensors, eliminating the need for explicit state estimation. SCDP decouples sensing from supervision through mixed-observation training: diffusion model conditions on sensor histories while being supervised to predict privileged future state-action trajectories, enforcing the model to infer the motion dynamics under partial observability. We further develop restricted denoising, context distribution alignment, and context-aware attention masking to encourage implicit state estimation within the model and to prevent train-deploy mismatch. We validate SCDP on velocity-commanded locomotion and motion reference tracking tasks. In simulation, SCDP achieves near-perfect success on velocity control (99-100%) and 93% tracking success in AMASS test set, performing comparable to privileged baselines while using only onboard sensors. Finally, we deploy the trained policy on a real G1 humanoid at 50 Hz, demonstrating robust real robot locomotion without external sensing or state estimation.

</details>


### [193] [Towards Terrain-Aware Safe Locomotion for Quadrupedal Robots Using Proprioceptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09585)
*Peiyu Yang,Jiatao Ding,Wei Pan,Claudio Semini,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于四足机器人在不平坦地形上安全行走的估计与控制框架，仅使用本体感知传感器（IMU、关节编码器、接触力传感器），通过2.5D地形图生成、支撑面参数提取，并结合安全临界控制屏障函数确保全局和局部安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四足机器人在真实不平坦地形中行走时的安全控制问题，特别是对于仅配备本体感知传感器的低成本机器人，需要可靠的估计方法和基于估计信息的安全临界控制。

Method: 1) 提出估计框架：生成2.5D地形图并提取支撑面参数，将其整合到接触和状态估计中；2) 将估计框架集成到安全临界控制管道中：通过控制屏障函数（CBF）提供严格的安全保证，利用历史地形信息和当前本体感知测量确保全局安全（避开危险区域）和局部安全（防止身体与地形碰撞）。

Result: 地形估计方法提供平滑的地形表示；耦合的地形-状态-接触估计框架相比解耦框架，将基座位置估计的平均绝对误差降低64.8%，估计方差降低47.2%，并提高了接触估计的鲁棒性；基于地形信息的CBF仅依靠本体感知即可确保全局和局部安全。

Conclusion: 该工作为仅配备本体感知传感器的四足机器人提供了一种在不平坦地形上实现安全行走的完整解决方案，通过耦合的估计框架显著提高了状态估计精度，并通过安全临界控制屏障函数提供了严格的安全保证。

Abstract: Achieving safe quadrupedal locomotion in real-world environments has attracted much attention in recent years. When walking over uneven terrain, achieving reliable estimation and realising safety-critical control based on the obtained information is still an open question. To address this challenge, especially for low-cost robots equipped solely with proprioceptive sensors (e.g., IMUs, joint encoders, and contact force sensors), this work first presents an estimation framework that generates a 2.5-D terrain map and extracts support plane parameters, which are then integrated into contact and state estimation. Then, we integrate this estimation framework into a safety-critical control pipeline by formulating control barrier functions that provide rigorous safety guarantees. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed terrain estimation method provides smooth terrain representations. Moreover, the coupled estimation framework of terrain, state, and contact reduces the mean absolute error of base position estimation by 64.8%, decreases the estimation variance by 47.2%, and improves the robustness of contact estimation compared to a decoupled framework. The terrain-informed CBFs integrate historical terrain information and current proprioceptive measurements to ensure global safety by keeping the robot out of hazardous areas and local safety by preventing body-terrain collision, relying solely on proprioceptive sensing.

</details>


### [194] [A Generalized Voronoi Graph based Coverage Control Approach for Non-Convex Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09596)
*Zuyi Guo,Ronghao Zheng,Meiqin Liu,Senlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于广义Voronoi图的多机器人覆盖控制方法，包含负载均衡和协作覆盖两个阶段，用于非凸多障碍物区域的高效覆盖


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多机器人系统在非凸区域（含多个障碍物）中高效覆盖的挑战，传统方法难以处理复杂几何形状和负载均衡问题

Method: 基于广义Voronoi图将非凸区域划分为多个子区域；提出加权负载均衡算法，考虑子区域质量差异，通过迭代优化机器人分配比例实现负载均衡；设计新控制器实现协作覆盖

Result: 证明了方法的收敛性，并通过仿真验证了性能，实现了非凸多障碍物区域的高效覆盖

Conclusion: 提出的基于GVG的覆盖控制方法能有效解决非凸多障碍物区域的机器人覆盖问题，通过负载均衡和协作控制实现高效覆盖

Abstract: To address the challenge of efficient coverage by multi-robot systems in non-convex regions with multiple obstacles, this paper proposes a coverage control method based on the Generalized Voronoi Graph (GVG), which has two phases: Load-Balancing Algorithm phase and Collaborative Coverage phase. In Load-Balancing Algorithm phase, the non-convex region is partitioned into multiple sub-regions based on GVG. Besides, a weighted load-balancing algorithm is developed, which considers the quality differences among sub-regions. By iteratively optimizing the robot allocation ratio, the number of robots in each sub-region is matched with the sub-region quality to achieve load balance. In Collaborative Coverage phase, each robot is controlled by a new controller to effectively coverage the region. The convergence of the method is proved and its performance is evaluated through simulations.

</details>


### [195] [DRIFT: Dual-Representation Inter-Fusion Transformer for Automated Driving Perception with 4D Radar Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09695)
*Siqi Pei,Andras Palffy,Dariu M. Gavrila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT提出了一种用于4D雷达点云的双路径架构，通过点路径和体素路径分别捕捉局部和全局特征，并通过特征共享层进行融合，在目标检测和自由道路估计任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达相比激光雷达点云密度显著更低，因此需要同时利用局部和全局上下文场景信息来提高感知性能。

Method: 提出DRIFT模型，采用双路径架构：点路径聚合细粒度局部特征，体素路径编码粗粒度全局特征；通过多阶段特征共享层将两条路径交织融合。

Result: 在View-of-Delft数据集和内部数据集上评估，在目标检测和/或自由道路估计任务上优于基线方法。在VoD数据集上达到52.6% mAP（相比CenterPoint的45.4%）。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过有效融合局部和全局特征，解决了4D雷达点云稀疏性问题，在自动驾驶感知任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: 4D radars, which provide 3D point cloud data along with Doppler velocity, are attractive components of modern automated driving systems due to their low cost and robustness under adverse weather conditions. However, they provide a significantly lower point cloud density than LiDAR sensors. This makes it important to exploit not only local but also global contextual scene information. This paper proposes DRIFT, a model that effectively captures and fuses both local and global contexts through a dual-path architecture. The model incorporates a point path to aggregate fine-grained local features and a pillar path to encode coarse-grained global features. These two parallel paths are intertwined via novel feature-sharing layers at multiple stages, enabling full utilization of both representations. DRIFT is evaluated on the widely used View-of-Delft (VoD) dataset and a proprietary internal dataset. It outperforms the baselines on the tasks of object detection and/or free road estimation. For example, DRIFT achieves a mean average precision (mAP) of 52.6\% (compared to, say, 45.4\% of CenterPoint) on the VoD dataset.

</details>


### [196] [Robotic Scene Cloning:Advancing Zero-Shot Robotic Scene Adaptation in Manipulation via Visual Prompt Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09712)
*Binyuan Huang,Yuqing Wen,Yucheng Zhao,Yaosi Hu,Tiancai Wang,Chang Wen Chen,Haoqiang Fan,Zhenzhong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RSC是一种通过编辑现有机器人操作轨迹实现场景特定适应的新方法，利用视觉提示机制和条件注入模块生成准确、场景一致的样本，显著提升策略在目标环境中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的机器人模型在现实用户场景中部署困难，因为其零样本能力有限，通常需要大量现场数据收集。需要一种方法来解决场景特定适应问题。

Method: 提出Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC)方法，通过编辑现有机器人操作轨迹实现场景特定适应。该方法利用视觉提示机制和精心调整的条件注入模块，不仅转移纹理，还根据视觉提示进行适度的形状适应。

Result: RSC在各种模拟和现实环境中表现出可靠的任务性能，能够处理多种对象类型。实验表明，RSC显著增强了策略在目标环境中的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RSC为解决预训练机器人模型在现实场景部署中的零样本能力限制问题提供了一种有效的场景特定适应方法，通过轨迹编辑和视觉提示机制实现了准确、场景一致的样本生成。

Abstract: Modern robots can perform a wide range of simple tasks and adapt to diverse scenarios in the well-trained environment. However, deploying pre-trained robot models in real-world user scenarios remains challenging due to their limited zero-shot capabilities, often necessitating extensive on-site data collection. To address this issue, we propose Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC), a novel method designed for scene-specific adaptation by editing existing robot operation trajectories. RSC achieves accurate and scene-consistent sample generation by leveraging a visual prompting mechanism and a carefully tuned condition injection module. Not only transferring textures but also performing moderate shape adaptations in response to the visual prompts, RSC demonstrates reliable task performance across a variety of object types. Experiments across various simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that RSC significantly enhances policy generalization in target environments.

</details>


### [197] [Let's Reward Step-by-Step: Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment for Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09740)
*Haoyuan Li,Rui Liu,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SACA框架通过感知基础步感知审计器和场景条件分组构建机制，从失败轨迹中提取密集监督，解决了VLN-CE中强化微调方法的稀疏奖励问题和模仿学习的复合误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLN-CE训练范式存在两个主要问题：1) 基于监督微调的策略在分布外状态下难以恢复，易产生复合误差；2) 强化微调方法受限于稀疏结果奖励，二元反馈无法为单个步骤分配信用，导致失败主导批次中的梯度信号崩溃。

Method: 提出Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment (SACA)框架：1) Perception-Grounded Step-Aware审计器逐步评估进展，将失败轨迹解耦为有效前缀和精确分歧点；2) Scenario-Conditioned Group Construction机制动态路由批次到专门的重新采样和优化策略，从非完美轨迹中提取密集监督信号。

Result: 在VLN-CE基准测试上的广泛实验表明，SACA实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SACA框架通过从失败轨迹中提取密集监督，有效解决了VLN-CE中强化微调的稀疏奖励问题和模仿学习的复合误差问题，显著提升了导航性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to learn complex reasoning from long-horizon human interactions. While Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have driven recent progress, current training paradigms struggle to balance generalization capability, error recovery and training stability. Specifically, (i) policies derived from SFT suffer from compounding errors, struggling to recover from out-of-distribution states, and (ii) Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) methods e.g. GRPO are bottlenecked by sparse outcome rewards. Their binary feedback fails to assign credit to individual steps, leading to gradient signal collapse in failure dominant batches. To address these challenges, we introduce Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment (SACA), a framework designed to extract dense supervision from imperfect trajectories. At its core, the Perception-Grounded Step-Aware auditor evaluates progress step-by-step, disentangling failed trajectories into valid prefixes and exact divergence points. Leveraging these signals, Scenario-Conditioned Group Construction mechanism dynamically routes batches to specialized resampling and optimization strategies. Extensive experiments on VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate that SACA achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [198] [Caterpillar-Inspired Spring-Based Compressive Continuum Robot for Bristle-based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09745)
*Zhixian Hu,Yu She,Juan Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于弹簧的肌腱驱动连续体机器人，用于受限空间检测，集成商业机械臂，结合人工刚毛接触传感器实现表面感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性机器人在管道、风道等受限空间探索中面临空间有限、几何不规则和访问受限等挑战，需要更灵活、紧凑的解决方案

Method: 设计紧凑的弹簧基肌腱驱动连续体机器人，集成商业机械臂，采用机械柔顺连续体结构结合肌腱驱动模块，实现弯曲和轴向长度变化的耦合运动，使用恒定曲率运动学模型进行位置控制

Result: 在提出的模型和控制管道下，平均位置误差为4.32毫米；集成人工刚毛接触传感器，成功演示了表面感知和受限空间探索

Conclusion: 该紧凑柔顺设计为商业机器人提供了经济有效的升级方案，有望在挑战性环境中实现有效的探索

Abstract: Exploration of confined spaces, such as pipelines and ducts, remains challenging for conventional rigid robots due to limited space, irregular geometry, and restricted access. Inspired by caterpillar locomotion and sensing, this paper presents a compact spring-based tendon-driven continuum robot that integrates with commercial robotic arms for confined-space inspection. The system combines a mechanically compliant continuum body with a tendon actuation module, enabling coupled bending and axial length change, and uses a constant-curvature kinematic model for positional control. Experiments show a mean position error of 4.32 mm under the proposed model and control pipeline. To extend the system from motion to inspection, we integrate an artificial bristle contact sensor and demonstrate surface perception and confined-space exploration through contact interactions. This compact and compliant design offers a cost-effective upgrade for commercial robots and promises effective exploration in challenging environments.

</details>


### [199] [TIMID: Time-Dependent Mistake Detection in Videos of Robot Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09782)
*Nerea Gallego,Fernando Salanova,Claudio Mannarano,Cristian Mahulea,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TIMID：一种基于视频异常检测的架构，用于检测机器人执行高级任务时的时间依赖错误，通过弱监督训练和模拟数据集解决数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测（VAD）框架主要关注单一的低级运动或动作失败，难以检测复杂的时空任务违规，因为这些违规不一定表现为低级执行错误。机器人系统执行越来越复杂的任务序列时，失败方式也越来越多样化。

Method: 提出TIMID架构，接收视频、任务提示和潜在错误提示作为输入，输出帧级预测判断错误是否存在。采用VAD公式化实现弱监督训练（每个视频只需单个标签）。为解决错误执行数据稀缺问题，引入包含受控时间错误的多机器人模拟数据集和真实执行数据，用于零样本模拟到真实评估。

Result: 实验表明，现成的视觉语言模型（VLM）缺乏此任务所需的显式时间推理能力，而TIMID框架成功检测了不同类型的时间错误。

Conclusion: TIMID架构能够有效检测机器人执行高级任务时的时间依赖错误，通过弱监督训练和模拟数据解决了传统VAD框架在复杂时空违规检测上的局限性。

Abstract: As robotic systems execute increasingly difficult task sequences, so does the number of ways in which they can fail. Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) frameworks typically focus on singular, low-level kinematic or action failures, struggling to identify more complex temporal or spatial task violations, because they do not necessarily manifest as low-level execution errors. To address this problem, the main contribution of this paper is a new VAD-inspired architecture, TIMID, which is able to detect robot time-dependent mistakes when executing high-level tasks. Our architecture receives as inputs a video and prompts of the task and the potential mistake, and returns a frame-level prediction in the video of whether the mistake is present or not. By adopting a VAD formulation, the model can be trained with weak supervision, requiring only a single label per video. Additionally, to alleviate the problem of data scarcity of incorrect executions, we introduce a multi-robot simulation dataset with controlled temporal errors and real executions for zero-shot sim-to-real evaluation. Our experiments demonstrate that out-of-the-box VLMs lack the explicit temporal reasoning required for this task, whereas our framework successfully detects different types of temporal errors. Project: https://ropertunizar.github.io/TIMID/

</details>


### [200] [Lightweight 3D LiDAR-Based UAV Tracking: An Adaptive Extended Kalman Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09783)
*Nivand Khosravi,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于轻量级3D LiDAR的无人机跟踪系统，采用自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器(AEKF)框架，解决稀疏、噪声和非均匀点云数据的挑战，适用于小型无人机在GPS拒止环境下的相对定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机群协同飞行和避障需要精确的相对定位。虽然视觉跟踪已得到广泛研究，但基于3D LiDAR的方法在变化光照条件下更鲁棒，却未被充分利用。现有系统通常依赖笨重、高功耗的传感器，不适合具有严格载荷和能量限制的小型无人机。

Method: 提出轻量级LiDAR无人机跟踪系统，采用自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器(AEKF)框架。该方法动态调整噪声协方差矩阵（使用创新和残差统计），增强真实条件下的跟踪精度。包含恢复机制，确保在LiDAR返回散射或遮挡导致的临时检测失败期间跟踪连续性。使用Livox Mid-360 LiDAR安装在DJI F550无人机上进行实验验证。

Result: 在真实飞行场景中，该方法在稀疏LiDAR返回和间歇检测下表现出鲁棒的无人机跟踪性能。在激进机动期间，始终优于标准卡尔曼滤波和粒子滤波方法。框架能够在GPS拒止环境中实现可靠的相对定位，无需多传感器阵列或外部基础设施。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器框架有效解决了非重复扫描3D LiDAR产生的稀疏、噪声和非均匀点云数据的挑战，为小型无人机提供了轻量级、可靠的相对定位解决方案，在GPS拒止环境中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Accurate relative positioning is crucial for swarm aerial robotics, enabling coordinated flight and collision avoidance. Although vision-based tracking has been extensively studied, 3D LiDAR-based methods remain underutilized despite their robustness under varying lighting conditions. Existing systems often rely on bulky, power-intensive sensors, making them impractical for small UAVs with strict payload and energy constraints. This paper presents a lightweight LiDAR-based UAV tracking system incorporating an Adaptive Extended Kalman Filter (AEKF) framework. Our approach effectively addresses the challenges posed by sparse, noisy, and nonuniform point cloud data generated by non-repetitive scanning 3D LiDARs, ensuring reliable tracking while remaining suitable for small drones with strict payload constraints. Unlike conventional filtering techniques, the proposed method dynamically adjusts the noise covariance matrices using innovation and residual statistics, thereby enhancing tracking accuracy under real-world conditions. Additionally, a recovery mechanism ensures continuity of tracking during temporary detection failures caused by scattered LiDAR returns or occlusions. Experimental validation was performed using a Livox Mid-360 LiDAR mounted on a DJI F550 UAV in real-world flight scenarios. The proposed method demonstrated robust UAV tracking performance under sparse LiDAR returns and intermittent detections, consistently outperforming both standard Kalman filtering and particle filtering approaches during aggressive maneuvers. These results confirm that the framework enables reliable relative positioning in GPS-denied environments without the need for multi-sensor arrays or external infrastructure.

</details>


### [201] [Emerging Extrinsic Dexterity in Cluttered Scenes via Dynamics-aware Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09882)
*Yixin Zheng,Jiangran Lyu,Yifan Zhang,Jiayi Chen,Mi Yan,Yuntian Deng,Xuesong Shi,Xiaoguang Zhao,Yizhou Wang,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAPL框架，通过显式世界建模学习接触诱导的物体动力学表示，用于条件强化学习，实现杂乱环境中的非抓握操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对杂乱环境中多物体交互复杂动力学的显式建模，限制了非抓握操作在实际环境中的应用

Method: DAPL框架：通过显式世界建模学习接触诱导的物体动力学表示，将该表示作为条件输入强化学习，无需手工接触启发式或复杂奖励设计

Result: 在模拟杂乱场景中比抓握操作、人工遥操作和先前基于表示的方法成功率高出25%以上；真实世界10个杂乱场景成功率约50%，杂货部署验证了鲁棒的模拟到真实迁移

Conclusion: DAPL框架通过显式建模接触动力学，有效实现了杂乱环境中的外在灵巧性，展示了实际应用的潜力

Abstract: Extrinsic dexterity leverages environmental contact to overcome the limitations of prehensile manipulation. However, achieving such dexterity in cluttered scenes remains challenging and underexplored, as it requires selectively exploiting contact among multiple interacting objects with inherently coupled dynamics. Existing approaches lack explicit modeling of such complex dynamics and therefore fall short in non-prehensile manipulation in cluttered environments, which in turn limits their practical applicability in real-world environments. In this paper, we introduce a Dynamics-Aware Policy Learning (DAPL) framework that can facilitate policy learning with a learned representation of contact-induced object dynamics in cluttered environments. This representation is learned through explicit world modeling and used to condition reinforcement learning, enabling extrinsic dexterity to emerge without hand-crafted contact heuristics or complex reward shaping. We evaluate our approach in both simulation and the real world. Our method outperforms prehensile manipulation, human teleoperation, and prior representation-based policies by over 25% in success rate on unseen simulated cluttered scenes with varying densities. The real-world success rate reaches around 50% across 10 cluttered scenes, while a practical grocery deployment further demonstrates robust sim-to-real transfer and applicability.

</details>


### [202] [Robust Cooperative Localization in Featureless Environments: A Comparative Study of DCL, StCL, CCL, CI, and Standard-CL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09886)
*Nivand Khosravi,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较五种协作定位方法在GPS拒止环境下的性能，发现CI方法在一致性和精度间取得最佳平衡，DCL在挑战条件下最稳定，而StCL和Standard-CL精度最高但一致性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人系统于GPS拒止环境中运行时，需要准确的协作定位方法。现有多种CL方法，但缺乏系统性的比较研究来指导实际应用选择。

Method: 在ROS中实现五种CL方法（CCL、DCL、StCL、CI、Standard-CL），通过蒙特卡洛模拟在两种条件下评估：弱数据关联和鲁棒检测。

Result: StCL和Standard-CL定位误差最低但滤波器一致性严重不足；DCL在挑战条件下稳定性最好；CI在一致性和精度间取得最佳平衡；CCL理论上最优但对异常值敏感。

Conclusion: 不同CL方法存在基本权衡，CI是最平衡的方法，DCL在挑战条件下最稳定，研究结果为基于应用需求选择CL算法提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Cooperative localization (CL) enables accurate position estimation in multi-robot systems operating in GPS-denied environments. This paper presents a comparative study of five CL approaches: Centralized Cooperative Localization (CCL), Decentralized Cooperative Localization (DCL), Sequential Cooperative Localization (StCL), Covariance Intersection (CI), and Standard Cooperative Localization (Standard-CL). All methods are implemented in ROS and evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations under two conditions: weak data association and robust detection. Our analysis reveals fundamental trade-offs among the methods. StCL and Standard-CL achieve the lowest position errors but exhibit severe filter inconsistency, making them unsuitable for safety-critical applications. DCL demonstrates remarkable stability under challenging conditions due to its measurement stride mechanism, which provides implicit regularization against outliers. CI emerges as the most balanced approach, achieving near-optimal consistency while maintaining competitive accuracy. CCL provides theoretically optimal estimation but shows sensitivity to measurement outliers. These findings offer practical guidance for selecting CL algorithms based on application requirements.

</details>


### [203] [NanoBench: A Multi-Task Benchmark Dataset for Nano-Quadrotor System Identification, Control, and State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09908)
*Syed Izzat Ullah,Jose Baca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NanoBench是一个针对27克纳米四旋翼飞行器（Crazyflie 2.1）的开源多任务基准数据集，包含170多条飞行轨迹，提供执行器级信号、控制器内部状态和毫米级精度地面真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中机器人基准主要针对数百克至数公斤的飞行器，仅提供高级状态数据，缺乏研究纳米级四旋翼所需的执行器级信号。纳米级飞行器的低雷诺数空气动力学、无芯直流电机非线性和严重计算约束使大型飞行器的模型和控制器失效。

Method: 在Vicon运动捕捉系统中使用Crazyflie 2.1纳米四旋翼（起飞重量27克）收集数据集，包含170多条飞行轨迹，涵盖悬停、多频激励、标准跟踪和激进机动等多种速度状态。数据以100Hz频率同步提供Vicon地面真值、原始IMU数据、机载扩展卡尔曼滤波器估计、PID控制器内部状态和电机PWM命令，以及10Hz的电池遥测数据，对齐一致性优于0.5毫秒。

Result: 创建了首个公开数据集，在商用纳米级空中平台上联合提供执行器命令、控制器内部状态和估计器输出，并具有毫米级精度地面真值。定义了三个任务的标准评估协议、训练/测试划分和开源基线：非线性系统辨识、闭环控制器基准测试和机载状态估计评估。

Conclusion: NanoBench填补了纳米级四旋翼飞行器研究的空白，为研究低雷诺数空气动力学、电机非线性和计算约束下的控制问题提供了首个全面的基准数据集，将促进纳米级空中机器人技术的发展。

Abstract: Existing aerial-robotics benchmarks target vehicles from hundreds of grams to several kilograms and typically expose only high-level state data. They omit the actuator-level signals required to study nano-scale quadrotors, where low-Reynolds number aerodynamics, coreless DC motor nonlinearities, and severe computational constraints invalidate models and controllers developed for larger vehicles. We introduce NanoBench, an open-source multi-task benchmark collected on the commercially available Crazyflie 2.1 nano-quadrotor (takeoff weight 27 g) in a Vicon motion capture arena. The dataset contains over 170 flight trajectories spanning hover, multi-frequency excitation, standard tracking, and aggressive maneuvers across multiple speed regimes. Each trajectory provides synchronized Vicon ground truth, raw IMU data, onboard extended Kalman filter estimates, PID controller internals, and motor PWM commands at 100 Hz, alongside battery telemetry at 10 Hz, aligned with sub-0.5 ms consistency. NanoBench defines standardized evaluation protocols, train/test splits, and open-source baselines for three tasks: nonlinear system identification, closed-loop controller benchmarking, and onboard state estimation assessment. To our knowledge, it is the first public dataset to jointly provide actuator commands, controller internals, and estimator outputs with millimeter-accurate ground truth on a commercially available nano-scale aerial platform.

</details>


### [204] [Kinodynamic Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Locomotion via Multi-Contact Whole-Body Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09956)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Steven Haener,Varun Madabushi,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KDMR框架通过多接触全身轨迹优化，结合运动捕捉和地面反作用力数据，解决传统运动重定向方法的物理不一致问题，提升下游模仿学习策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动捕捉的运动重定向方法仅依赖空间运动数据，导致物理不一致的伪影（如脚部滑动、地面穿透），严重降低下游模仿学习策略的性能。需要一种能确保动态可行性的重定向方法。

Method: KDMR将运动重定向建模为多接触全身轨迹优化问题，通过显式强制执行刚体动力学和接触互补约束，结合运动捕捉数据和地面反作用力测量，自动检测脚跟-脚趾接触事件以准确复制复杂的人类接触模式。

Result: KDMR在三个关键维度上显著优于最先进的基线GMR：1) 重定向运动的动态可行性和平滑性；2) 与原始源数据相比的地面反作用力跟踪精度；3) 通过BeyondMimic框架训练的下游控制策略的训练效率和最终性能。实验表明KDMR产生动态可行的参考轨迹，加速策略收敛并增强整体运动稳定性。

Conclusion: KDMR框架通过整合动力学约束和接触力测量，解决了传统运动重定向方法的物理不一致问题，为下游模仿学习提供了高质量的动态可行参考轨迹，显著提升策略性能。该端到端管道将在发表后开源。

Abstract: We present the KinoDynamic Motion Retargeting (KDMR) framework, a novel approach for humanoid locomotion that models the retargeting process as a multi-contact, whole-body trajectory optimization problem. Conventional kinematics-based retargeting methods rely solely on spatial motion capture (MoCap) data, inevitably introducing physically inconsistent artifacts, such as foot sliding and ground penetration, that severely degrade the performance of downstream imitation learning policies. To bridge this gap, KDMR extends beyond pure kinematics by explicitly enforcing rigid-body dynamics and contact complementarity constraints. Further, by integrating ground reaction force (GRF) measurements alongside MoCap data, our method automatically detects heel-toe contact events to accurately replicate complex human-like contact patterns. We evaluate KDMR against the state-of-the-art baseline, GMR, across three key dimensions: 1) the dynamic feasibility and smoothness of the retargeted motions, 2) the accuracy of GRF tracking compared to raw source data, and 3) the training efficiency and final performance of downstream control policies trained via the BeyondMimic framework. Experimental results demonstrate that KDMR significantly outperforms purely kinematic methods, yielding dynamically viable reference trajectories that accelerate policy convergence and enhance overall locomotion stability. Our end-to-end pipeline will be open-sourced upon publication.

</details>


### [205] [BEACON: Language-Conditioned Navigation Affordance Prediction under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09961)
*Xinyu Gao,Gang Chen,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BEACON：一种用于语言条件局部导航的鸟瞰图空间预测方法，通过预测包含遮挡区域的BEV可通行热图，解决了现有图像空间方法在遮挡区域定位的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的方法主要在图像空间进行推理，产生与可见像素绑定的2D预测，难以推断被家具或移动人类遮挡区域的目标位置，这限制了机器人导航的实用性。

Method: BEACON预测以机器人为中心的鸟瞰图可通行热图，覆盖包含遮挡区域的局部区域。方法结合四方向环绕RGB-D观测和语言指令，通过向VLM注入空间线索，并将VLM输出与深度导出的BEV特征融合来预测BEV热图。

Result: 在Habitat模拟器中构建的遮挡感知数据集上进行实验，BEACON在验证子集上（包含遮挡目标位置）相对于最先进的图像空间基线，在测地线阈值平均准确率上提高了22.74个百分点。

Conclusion: BEV空间表示比图像空间方法更有效地处理遮挡区域的目标定位，BEACON通过融合视觉语言模型和深度特征，显著提升了语言条件局部导航在遮挡场景下的性能。

Abstract: Language-conditioned local navigation requires a robot to infer a nearby traversable target location from its current observation and an open-vocabulary, relational instruction. Existing vision-language spatial grounding methods usually rely on vision-language models (VLMs) to reason in image space, producing 2D predictions tied to visible pixels. As a result, they struggle to infer target locations in occluded regions, typically caused by furniture or moving humans. To address this issue, we propose BEACON, which predicts an ego-centric Bird's-Eye View (BEV) affordance heatmap over a bounded local region including occluded areas. Given an instruction and surround-view RGB-D observations from four directions around the robot, BEACON predicts the BEV heatmap by injecting spatial cues into a VLM and fusing the VLM's output with depth-derived BEV features. Using an occlusion-aware dataset built in the Habitat simulator, we conduct detailed experimental analysis to validate both our BEV space formulation and the design choices of each module. Our method improves the accuracy averaged across geodesic thresholds by 22.74 percentage points over the state-of-the-art image-space baseline on the validation subset with occluded target locations. Our project page is: https://xin-yu-gao.github.io/beacon.

</details>


### [206] [TiPToP: A Modular Open-Vocabulary Planning System for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09971)
*William Shen,Nishanth Kumar,Sahit Chintalapudi,Jie Wang,Christopher Watson,Edward Hu,Jing Cao,Dinesh Jayaraman,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Tomás Lozano-Pérez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TiPToP是一个结合预训练视觉基础模型与现有任务运动规划器的模块化系统，能够直接从RGB图像和自然语言指令解决多步操作任务，无需机器人数据，性能优于经过350小时演示微调的VLA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的机器人系统通常需要大量机器人特定数据，而模块化系统虽然灵活但集成复杂。TiPToP旨在开发一个简单易用、无需机器人数据、能快速部署到新机器人平台的模块化系统，实现学习与规划的紧密集成。

Method: TiPToP采用模块化架构，将预训练视觉基础模型与现有任务运动规划器(TAMP)结合。系统从RGB图像和自然语言指令直接工作，可在一小时内部署到标准DROID设置，并能以最小努力适配新机器人平台。

Result: 在28个桌面操作任务的仿真和真实世界评估中，TiPToP（零机器人数据）匹配或优于π0.5-DROID（经过350小时机器人特定演示微调的视觉语言动作模型）。通过173次试验的失败模式分析，识别了系统改进方向。

Conclusion: TiPToP展示了模块化系统在无需机器人数据的情况下实现高性能操作任务的潜力，为学习与规划的紧密集成研究提供了开源平台，推动了模块化操作系统的发展。

Abstract: We present TiPToP, an extensible modular system that combines pretrained vision foundation models with an existing Task and Motion Planner (TAMP) to solve multi-step manipulation tasks directly from input RGB images and natural-language instructions. Our system aims to be simple and easy-to-use: it can be installed and run on a standard DROID setup in under one hour and adapted to new embodiments with minimal effort. We evaluate TiPToP -- which requires zero robot data -- over 28 tabletop manipulation tasks in simulation and the real world and find it matches or outperforms $π_{0.5}\text{-DROID}$, a vision-language-action (VLA) model fine-tuned on 350 hours of embodiment-specific demonstrations. TiPToP's modular architecture enables us to analyze the system's failure modes at the component level. We analyze results from an evaluation of 173 trials and identify directions for improvement. We release TiPToP open-source to further research on modular manipulation systems and tighter integration between learning and planning. Project website and code: https://tiptop-robot.github.io

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [MASEval: Extending Multi-Agent Evaluation from Models to Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08835)
*Cornelius Emde,Alexander Rubinstein,Anmol Goel,Ahmed Heakl,Sangdoo Yun,Seong Joon Oh,Martin Gubri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASEval是一个框架无关的评估库，用于系统级比较LLM智能体系统，发现框架选择对性能的影响与模型选择同样重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注模型本身，固定智能体设置而忽略其他系统组件。实际中，实现决策（如拓扑结构、编排逻辑、错误处理）对性能有显著影响，但缺乏相应的评估方法。

Method: 开发了框架无关的MASEval库，将整个系统作为分析单元。通过系统级比较，涵盖3个基准测试、3个模型和3个框架，全面评估不同组件对性能的影响。

Result: 研究发现框架选择对性能的影响与模型选择同样重要。MASEval能够帮助研究人员探索智能体系统的所有组件，为系统设计提供新思路，并帮助实践者选择最佳实现方案。

Conclusion: MASEval填补了智能体系统评估的空白，提供了系统级的分析框架，促进了智能体系统的原则性设计和实践应用优化。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of LLM-based agentic systems has produced a rich ecosystem of frameworks (smolagents, LangGraph, AutoGen, CAMEL, LlamaIndex, i.a.). Yet existing benchmarks are model-centric: they fix the agentic setup and do not compare other system components. We argue that implementation decisions substantially impact performance, including choices such as topology, orchestration logic, and error handling. MASEval addresses this evaluation gap with a framework-agnostic library that treats the entire system as the unit of analysis. Through a systematic system-level comparison across 3 benchmarks, 3 models, and 3 frameworks, we find that framework choice matters as much as model choice. MASEval allows researchers to explore all components of agentic systems, opening new avenues for principled system design, and practitioners to identify the best implementation for their use case.
  MASEval is available under the MIT licence https://github.com/parameterlab/MASEval.

</details>


### [208] [Quantifying the Accuracy and Cost Impact of Design Decisions in Budget-Constrained Agentic LLM Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08877)
*Kyle McCleary,James Ghawaly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 预算约束下智能RAG系统的测量研究：搜索深度、检索策略和完成预算对准确性和成本的影响分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能检索增强生成系统在部署环境中面临明确的工具调用和完成token预算约束，需要研究如何在固定约束下优化系统配置

Method: 提出BCAS评估框架，在六个LLM和三个QA基准上进行对比实验，分析搜索深度、检索策略和完成预算的影响

Result: 准确性随搜索次数增加而提升但存在上限；混合检索加轻量重排效果最佳；更大完成预算对HotpotQA类合成任务最有效

Conclusion: 为配置预算约束的智能检索管道提供实用指导，并提供了可复现的提示和评估设置

Abstract: Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems combine iterative search, planning prompts, and retrieval backends, but deployed settings impose explicit budgets on tool calls and completion tokens. We present a controlled measurement study of how search depth, retrieval strategy, and completion budget affect accuracy and cost under fixed constraints. Using Budget-Constrained Agentic Search (BCAS), a model-agnostic evaluation harness that surfaces remaining budget and gates tool use, we run comparisons across six LLMs and three question-answering benchmarks. Across models and datasets, accuracy improves with additional searches up to a small cap, hybrid lexical and dense retrieval with lightweight re-ranking produces the largest average gains in our ablation grid, and larger completion budgets are most helpful on HotpotQA-style synthesis. These results provide practical guidance for configuring budgeted agentic retrieval pipelines and are accompanied by reproducible prompts and evaluation settings.

</details>


### [209] [Interpretable Markov-Based Spatiotemporal Risk Surfaces for Missing-Child Search Planning with Reinforcement Learning and LLM-Based Quality Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08933)
*Joshua Castillo,Ravi Mukkamala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guardian是一个用于失踪儿童调查的端到端决策支持系统，通过三层架构（马尔可夫链、强化学习、LLM验证）将非结构化案件数据转化为可操作的72小时搜索计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失踪儿童案件的前72小时对成功找回至关重要，但执法机构面临数据碎片化、非结构化以及缺乏动态地理空间预测工具的挑战。需要系统化的决策支持系统来提高搜索效率。

Method: 系统采用三层预测架构：1）马尔可夫链模型，考虑道路可达性、隐蔽偏好、走廊偏差等时空因素，分白天/夜晚参数化；2）强化学习将预测分布转化为可操作的搜索计划；3）LLM对搜索计划进行事后验证。系统将非结构化案件文档转换为模式对齐的时空表示，并加入地理编码和交通上下文。

Result: 通过合成但真实的案例研究，系统在24/48/72小时时间范围内产生定量输出。结果显示，三层架构的预测系统能够为区域优化和人工审查生成可解释的先验概率分布。

Conclusion: Guardian系统为失踪儿童调查提供了有效的端到端决策支持，其三层预测架构结合了可解释性、操作实用性和验证机制，能够在前72小时关键期内生成实用的搜索计划。

Abstract: The first 72 hours of a missing-child investigation are critical for successful recovery. However, law enforcement agencies often face fragmented, unstructured data and a lack of dynamic, geospatial predictive tools. Our system, Guardian, provides an end-to-end decision-support system for missing-child investigation and early search planning. It converts heterogeneous, unstructured case documents into a schema-aligned spatiotemporal representation, enriches cases with geocoding and transportation context, and provides probabilistic search products spanning 0-72 hours. In this paper, we present an overview of Guardian as well as a detailed description of a three-layer predictive component of the system. The first layer is a Markov chain, a sparse, interpretable model with transitions incorporating road accessibility costs, seclusion preferences, and corridor bias with separate day/night parameterizations. The Markov chain's output prediction distributions are then transformed into operationally useful search plans by the second layer's reinforcement learning. Finally, the third layer's LLM performs post hoc validation of layer 2 search plans prior to their release. Using a synthetic but realistic case study, we report quantitative outputs across 24/48/72-hour horizons and analyze sensitivity, failure modes, and tradeoffs. Results show that the proposed predictive system with the three-layer architecture produces interpretable priors for zone optimization and human review.

</details>


### [210] [AgentOS: From Application Silos to a Natural Language-Driven Data Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08938)
*Rui Liu,Tao Zhe,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Kunpeng Liu,Yanjie Fu,Huan Liu,Jian Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出个人智能体操作系统（AgentOS）新范式，用自然用户界面替代传统GUI桌面，以智能体内核为核心协调多智能体，将传统应用转化为模块化技能，将操作系统构建视为知识发现与数据挖掘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体（如OpenClaw）虽能自主操作本地计算环境，但仍运行在传统GUI/CLI操作系统上，导致交互模型碎片化、权限管理混乱（"影子AI"）和严重上下文碎片化，存在架构不匹配问题。

Method: 提出AgentOS架构：1）用自然用户界面（NUI）替代传统GUI桌面；2）智能体内核负责解释用户意图、分解任务、协调多智能体；3）传统应用演变为模块化技能，用户可通过自然语言规则组合软件；4）将操作系统构建视为KDD问题，智能体内核作为实时意图挖掘和知识发现引擎。

Result: 将操作系统重新定义为连续数据挖掘管道，涉及工作流自动化的序列模式挖掘、技能检索的推荐系统、动态演化的个人知识图谱，为KDD社区定义了构建下一代智能计算系统的新研究议程。

Conclusion: 实现AgentOS从根本上成为知识发现与数据挖掘问题，需要将操作系统构建为实时意图挖掘和知识发现引擎，这为KDD社区在构建下一代智能计算系统方面开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of open-source, locally hosted intelligent agents marks a critical inflection point in human-computer interaction. Systems such as OpenClaw demonstrate that Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents can autonomously operate local computing environments, orchestrate workflows, and integrate external tools. However, within the current paradigm, these agents remain conventional applications running on legacy operating systems originally designed for Graphical User Interfaces (GUIs) or Command Line Interfaces (CLIs). This architectural mismatch leads to fragmented interaction models, poorly structured permission management (often described as "Shadow AI"), and severe context fragmentation. This paper proposes a new paradigm: a Personal Agent Operating System (AgentOS). In AgentOS, traditional GUI desktops are replaced by a Natural User Interface (NUI) centered on a unified natural language or voice portal. The system core becomes an Agent Kernel that interprets user intent, decomposes tasks, and coordinates multiple agents, while traditional applications evolve into modular Skills-as-Modules enabling users to compose software through natural language rules. We argue that realizing AgentOS fundamentally becomes a Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining (KDD) problem. The Agent Kernel must operate as a real-time engine for intent mining and knowledge discovery. Viewed through this lens, the operating system becomes a continuous data mining pipeline involving sequential pattern mining for workflow automation, recommender systems for skill retrieval, and dynamically evolving personal knowledge graphs. These challenges define a new research agenda for the KDD community in building the next generation of intelligent computing systems.

</details>


### [211] [A Consensus-Driven Multi-LLM Pipeline for Missing-Person Investigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08954)
*Joshua Castillo,Ravi Mukkamala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guardian LLM Pipeline是一个用于失踪儿童调查的多模型系统，利用LLM进行智能信息提取和处理，通过共识机制协调多个专门化模型，并采用QLoRA微调增强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失踪人员调查的前72小时对成功找回至关重要，需要高效的信息处理系统来支持早期搜索规划。

Method: 开发了一个端到端的多模型LLM流水线，协调任务专门化的LLM模型，采用共识LLM引擎比较多个模型输出并解决分歧，通过QLoRA微调使用精选数据集增强性能。

Result: 设计了一个支持失踪儿童调查和早期搜索规划的系统，强调将LLM作为结构化提取器和标注器使用，而非不受约束的端到端决策者。

Conclusion: Guardian LLM Pipeline通过多模型协调和共识机制，为失踪人员调查提供了可审计的LLM辅助信息处理框架，符合弱监督和LLM辅助标注的研究方向。

Abstract: The first 72 hours of a missing-person investigation are critical for successful recovery. Guardian is an end-to-end system designed to support missing-child investigation and early search planning. This paper presents the Guardian LLM Pipeline, a multi-model system in which LLMs are used for intelligent information extraction and processing related to missing-person search operations. The pipeline coordinates end-to-end execution across task-specialized LLM models and invokes a consensus LLM engine that compares multiple model outputs and resolves disagreements. The pipeline is further strengthened by QLoRA-based fine-tuning, using curated datasets. The presented design aligns with prior work on weak supervision and LLM-assisted annotation, emphasizing conservative, auditable use of LLMs as structured extractors and labelers rather than unconstrained end-to-end decision makers.

</details>


### [212] [The FABRIC Strategy for Verifying Neural Feedback Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08964)
*I. Samuel Akinwande,Sydney M. Katz,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Clark Barrett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FaBRIC算法，整合前向和后向可达性分析来验证神经反馈系统的可达-规避规范，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经反馈系统的后向可达性分析研究不足，现有技术可扩展性有限，需要开发更高效的后向可达性分析方法并与前向分析结合。

Method: 提出新算法计算非线性神经反馈系统后向可达集的过近似和欠近似，并将这些后向可达性技术与现有前向分析技术集成，形成FaBRIC算法。

Result: 在代表性基准测试中，新算法显著优于现有技术水平，证明了整合前向和后向可达性分析的有效性。

Conclusion: FaBRIC算法通过整合前向和后向可达性分析，为神经反馈系统的可达-规避规范验证提供了更有效的解决方案，填补了后向可达性分析研究的空白。

Abstract: Forward reachability analysis is a dominant approach for verifying reach-avoid specifications in neural feedback systems, i.e., dynamical systems controlled by neural networks, and a number of directions have been proposed and studied. In contrast, far less attention has been given to backward reachability analysis for these systems, in part because of the limited scalability of known techniques. In this work, we begin to address this gap by introducing new algorithms for computing both over- and underapproximations of backward reachable sets for nonlinear neural feedback systems. We also describe and implement an integration of these backward reachability techniques with existing ones for forward analysis. We call the resulting algorithm Forward and Backward Reachability Integration for Certification (FaBRIC). We evaluate our algorithms on a representative set of benchmarks and show that they significantly outperform the prior state of the art.

</details>


### [213] [Meissa: Multi-modal Medical Agentic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09018)
*Yixiong Chen,Xinyi Bai,Yue Pan,Zongwei Zhou,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Meissa是一个轻量级4B参数医疗多模态大语言模型，通过蒸馏前沿模型的结构化轨迹实现离线智能体能力，在医疗图像理解和临床推理任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗智能体系统过度依赖GPT等前沿模型，存在API部署成本高、延迟大、隐私风险等问题，与临床本地化需求冲突。需要开发轻量级离线医疗智能体模型。

Method: 提出统一轨迹建模将推理和行动轨迹表示为状态-动作-观察形式；三层分层监督根据模型错误逐步升级监督强度；前瞻-回顾监督结合探索性前向轨迹和事后合理化执行轨迹。

Result: 在13个医疗基准测试的16个评估场景中，Meissa在10个场景中匹配或超越专有前沿智能体；相比Gemini-3等模型参数减少25倍以上，端到端延迟降低22倍，完全离线运行。

Conclusion: Meissa通过结构化轨迹蒸馏实现了轻量级离线医疗智能体，在保持高性能的同时解决了成本、延迟和隐私问题，为临床部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MM-LLMs) have shown strong performance in medical image understanding and clinical reasoning. Recent medical agent systems extend them with tool use and multi-agent collaboration, enabling complex decision-making. However, these systems rely almost entirely on frontier models (e.g., GPT), whose API-based deployment incurs high cost, high latency, and privacy risks that conflict with on-premise clinical requirements. We present Meissa, a lightweight 4B-parameter medical MM-LLM that brings agentic capability offline. Instead of imitating static answers, Meissa learns both when to engage external interaction (strategy selection) and how to execute multi-step interaction (strategy execution) by distilling structured trajectories from frontier models. Specifically, we propose: (1) Unified trajectory modeling: trajectories (reasoning and action traces) are represented within a single state-action-observation formalism, allowing one model to generalize across heterogeneous medical environments. (2) Three-tier stratified supervision: the model's own errors trigger progressive escalation from direct reasoning to tool-augmented and multi-agent interaction, explicitly learning difficulty-aware strategy selection. (3) Prospective-retrospective supervision: pairing exploratory forward traces with hindsight-rationalized execution traces enables stable learning of effective interaction policies. Trained on 40K curated trajectories, Meissa matches or exceeds proprietary frontier agents in 10 of 16 evaluation settings across 13 medical benchmarks spanning radiology, pathology, and clinical reasoning. Using over 25x fewer parameters than typical frontier models like Gemini-3, Meissa operates fully offline with 22x lower end-to-end latency compared to API-based deployment. Data, models, and environments are released at https://github.com/Schuture/Meissa.

</details>


### [214] [MEMO: Memory-Augmented Model Context Optimization for Robust Multi-Turn Multi-Agent LLM Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09022)
*Yunfei Xie,Kevin Wang,Bobby Cheng,Jianzhu Yao,Zhizhou Sha,Alexander Duffy,Yihan Xi,Hongyuan Mei,Cheston Tan,Chen Wei,Pramod Viswanath,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MEMO框架通过记忆增强的上下文优化，解决多智能体LLM游戏评估中的高方差问题，提升胜率和稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮多智能体LLM游戏评估存在显著的运行间方差，早期微小偏差在多轮交互中被复合放大，导致胜率估计偏差和排名不可靠。提示选择进一步加剧了这一问题，产生了不同的有效策略。

Method: MEMO（记忆增强的模型上下文优化）是一个自对弈框架，通过耦合保留和探索来优化推理时上下文。保留机制维护持久记忆库，存储自对弈轨迹的结构化见解，并在后续对局中作为先验注入。探索机制运行锦标赛式提示演化，通过TrueSkill进行不确定性感知选择，并使用优先回放重新访问罕见和决定性状态。

Result: 在五个文本游戏中，MEMO将GPT-4o-mini的平均胜率从25.1%提升至49.5%，Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct从20.9%提升至44.3%，每个任务使用2000场自对弈游戏。运行间方差也显著下降，使不同提示变体下的排名更加稳定。在谈判和不完全信息游戏中获得最大提升，而RL在完全信息设置中仍然更有效。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM游戏性能和鲁棒性通过上下文优化有显著提升空间。MEMO框架通过记忆增强和探索机制有效解决了评估方差问题，为多智能体交互提供了更可靠的评估方法。

Abstract: Multi-turn, multi-agent LLM game evaluations often exhibit substantial run-to-run variance. In long-horizon interactions, small early deviations compound across turns and are amplified by multi-agent coupling. This biases win rate estimates and makes rankings unreliable across repeated tournaments. Prompt choice worsens this further by producing different effective policies. We address both instability and underperformance with MEMO (Memory-augmented MOdel context optimization), a self-play framework that optimizes inference-time context by coupling retention and exploration. Retention maintains a persistent memory bank that stores structured insights from self-play trajectories and injects them as priors during later play. Exploration runs tournament-style prompt evolution with uncertainty-aware selection via TrueSkill, and uses prioritized replay to revisit rare and decisive states. Across five text-based games, MEMO raises mean win rate from 25.1% to 49.5% for GPT-4o-mini and from 20.9% to 44.3% for Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, using $2,000$ self-play games per task. Run-to-run variance also drops, giving more stable rankings across prompt variations. These results suggest that multi-agent LLM game performance and robustness have substantial room for improvement through context optimization. MEMO achieves the largest gains in negotiation and imperfect-information games, while RL remains more effective in perfect-information settings.

</details>


### [215] [Deep Tabular Research via Continual Experience-Driven Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09151)
*Junnan Dong,Chuang Zhou,Zheng Yuan,Yifei Yu,Siyu An,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Feiyue Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Deep Tabular Research (DTR)框架，通过分层元图、期望感知选择策略和孪生结构化记忆，解决语言模型在非结构化表格长程推理中的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在处理具有分层双向表头和非规范布局的非结构化表格时，难以完成复杂的长程分析任务。需要解决表格推理中的多步推理和相互依赖区域分析问题。

Method: 1) 构建分层元图捕捉双向语义，将自然语言查询映射到操作级搜索空间；2) 引入期望感知选择策略优先选择高效用执行路径；3) 将历史执行结果合成孪生结构化记忆（参数化更新和抽象文本），实现持续优化。

Result: 在具有挑战性的非结构化表格基准测试中进行了广泛实验，验证了框架的有效性，并强调了将战略规划与低级执行分离对于长程表格推理的必要性。

Conclusion: 提出的DTR框架通过将表格推理建模为闭环决策过程，结合分层语义理解、智能路径选择和持续记忆更新，有效解决了非结构化表格的长程分析挑战。

Abstract: Large language models often struggle with complex long-horizon analytical tasks over unstructured tables, which typically feature hierarchical and bidirectional headers and non-canonical layouts. We formalize this challenge as Deep Tabular Research (DTR), requiring multi-step reasoning over interdependent table regions. To address DTR, we propose a novel agentic framework that treats tabular reasoning as a closed-loop decision-making process. We carefully design a coupled query and table comprehension for path decision making and operational execution. Specifically, (i) DTR first constructs a hierarchical meta graph to capture bidirectional semantics, mapping natural language queries into an operation-level search space; (ii) To navigate this space, we introduce an expectation-aware selection policy that prioritizes high-utility execution paths; (iii) Crucially, historical execution outcomes are synthesized into a siamese structured memory, i.e., parameterized updates and abstracted texts, enabling continual refinement. Extensive experiments on challenging unstructured tabular benchmarks verify the effectiveness and highlight the necessity of separating strategic planning from low-level execution for long-horizon tabular reasoning.

</details>


### [216] [DataFactory: Collaborative Multi-Agent Framework for Advanced Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09152)
*Tong Wang,Chi Jin,Yongkang Chen,Huan Deng,Xiaohui Kuang,Gang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataFactory是一个多智能体框架，通过专门团队协调和自动化知识转换解决TableQA中LLM的上下文限制、幻觉问题和单智能体架构不足，显著提升表格问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在表格问答中存在三个关键限制：上下文长度限制影响数据处理能力、幻觉问题损害答案可靠性、单智能体架构难以处理涉及语义关系和多重逻辑的复杂推理场景。

Method: 提出DataFactory多智能体框架，包含采用ReAct范式进行推理编排的数据领导者，以及专门的数据库和知识图谱团队，将复杂查询系统分解为结构化和关系推理任务。通过映射函数T:D×S×R→G实现自动化数据到知识图谱转换，采用自然语言咨询实现灵活智能体间协商和自适应规划，并应用上下文工程策略整合历史模式和领域知识。

Result: 在TabFact、WikiTableQuestions和FeTaQA三个数据集上，使用来自五个提供商的八个LLM，结果显示一致提升：TabFact准确率提升20.2%，WikiTQ提升23.9%（Cohen's d > 1）。团队协调优于单团队变体（TabFact +5.5%，WikiTQ +14.4%，FeTaQA ROUGE-2 +17.1%）。

Conclusion: DataFactory框架为多智能体协作提供了设计指南，通过集成结构化查询和图基知识表示，为企业数据分析提供了实用平台，有效解决了现有LLM在表格问答中的关键限制。

Abstract: Table Question Answering (TableQA) enables natural language interaction with structured tabular data. However, existing large language model (LLM) approaches face critical limitations: context length constraints that restrict data handling capabilities, hallucination issues that compromise answer reliability, and single-agent architectures that struggle with complex reasoning scenarios involving semantic relationships and multi-hop logic. This paper introduces DataFactory, a multi-agent framework that addresses these limitations through specialized team coordination and automated knowledge transformation. The framework comprises a Data Leader employing the ReAct paradigm for reasoning orchestration, together with dedicated Database and Knowledge Graph teams, enabling the systematic decomposition of complex queries into structured and relational reasoning tasks. We formalize automated data-to-knowledge graph transformation via the mapping function T:D x S x R -> G, and implement natural language-based consultation that - unlike fixed workflow multi-agent systems - enables flexible inter-agent deliberation and adaptive planning to improve coordination robustness. We also apply context engineering strategies that integrate historical patterns and domain knowledge to reduce hallucinations and improve query accuracy. Across TabFact, WikiTableQuestions, and FeTaQA, using eight LLMs from five providers, results show consistent gains. Our approach improves accuracy by 20.2% (TabFact) and 23.9% (WikiTQ) over baselines, with significant effects (Cohen's d > 1). Team coordination also outperforms single-team variants (+5.5% TabFact, +14.4% WikiTQ, +17.1% FeTaQA ROUGE-2). The framework offers design guidelines for multi-agent collaboration and a practical platform for enterprise data analysis through integrated structured querying and graph-based knowledge representation.

</details>


### [217] [The Reasoning Trap -- Logical Reasoning as a Mechanistic Pathway to Situational Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09200)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RAISE框架，揭示逻辑推理能力提升会通过三条机制路径（演绎自我推断、归纳情境识别、溯因自我建模）导致AI系统情境意识增强，形成从基本自我认知到战略欺骗的升级阶梯，并指出当前安全措施不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于两个看似独立的研究方向正在发生碰撞：一方面情境意识被认为是AI系统最危险的能力之一，另一方面逻辑推理能力提升是LLM研究的重要方向。作者担忧逻辑推理能力的进步会无意中增强AI的情境意识，带来安全风险。

Method: 作者提出RAISE框架，识别三条机制路径：1) 演绎自我推断 - 通过演绎推理推断自身性质；2) 归纳情境识别 - 通过归纳推理识别训练部署环境；3) 溯因自我建模 - 通过溯因推理建立自我模型。构建从基本自我认知到战略欺骗的升级阶梯，分析当前LLM逻辑推理研究如何映射到情境意识放大器。

Result: 分析表明所有主要LLM逻辑推理研究主题都直接对应特定的情境意识放大器，当前安全措施不足以阻止这种升级。逻辑推理能力的提升会系统性地增强AI系统的情境意识，形成潜在的安全风险升级路径。

Conclusion: 结论提出具体保障措施：包括"镜像测试"基准和推理安全对等原则，并向逻辑推理研究社区提出关于其在此轨迹中责任的必要问题。强调需要重新评估逻辑推理研究的安全影响，建立预防性安全框架。

Abstract: Situational awareness, the capacity of an AI system to recognize its own nature, understand its training and deployment context, and reason strategically about its circumstances, is widely considered among the most dangerous emergent capabilities in advanced AI systems. Separately, a growing research effort seeks to improve the logical reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) across deduction, induction, and abduction. In this paper, we argue that these two research trajectories are on a collision course. We introduce the RAISE framework (Reasoning Advancing Into Self Examination), which identifies three mechanistic pathways through which improvements in logical reasoning enable progressively deeper levels of situational awareness: deductive self inference, inductive context recognition, and abductive self modeling. We formalize each pathway, construct an escalation ladder from basic self recognition to strategic deception, and demonstrate that every major research topic in LLM logical reasoning maps directly onto a specific amplifier of situational awareness. We further analyze why current safety measures are insufficient to prevent this escalation. We conclude by proposing concrete safeguards, including a "Mirror Test" benchmark and a Reasoning Safety Parity Principle, and pose an uncomfortable but necessary question to the logical reasoning community about its responsibility in this trajectory.

</details>


### [218] [Cognitively Layered Data Synthesis for Domain Adaptation of LLMs to Space Situational Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09231)
*Ding Linghu,Cheng Wang,Da Fan,Wei Shi,Kaifeng Yin,Xiaoliang Xue,Fan Yang,Haiyi Ren,Cong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BD-FDG框架，通过结构化知识组织、认知分层问题建模和自动化质量控制构建高质量SFT数据集，应用于空间态势感知领域，显著提升领域性能同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用任务上表现优异，但在复杂工程领域（如空间态势感知）的迁移面临挑战，主要瓶颈在于高质量监督微调数据集的构建。现有方法存在知识覆盖不完整、认知深度不足、质量可控性有限等问题。

Method: 提出BD-FDG框架，包含三个机制：1) 结构化知识组织：使用知识树确保结构化语料覆盖；2) 认知分层问题建模：设计跨越9个类别和6个认知层次（从记忆到创造）的问题生成方案，产生连续难度梯度的样本；3) 自动化质量控制：应用多维评分管道确保领域严谨性和一致性。

Result: 使用BD-FDG构建了约23万样本的SSA-SFT数据集，微调Qwen3-8B得到SSA-LLM-8B。在领域测试集上，BLEU-1相对提升144%（无思考）和176%（有思考）；竞技场比较中胜率达82.21%；同时基本保持通用基准性能（MMLU-Pro, MATH-500）。

Conclusion: 认知分层驱动的SFT数据构建是复杂工程领域LLM适应的有效范式，BD-FDG框架为领域特定LLM适配提供了可迁移的方法论。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate exceptional performance on general-purpose tasks. however, transferring them to complex engineering domains such as space situational awareness (SSA) remains challenging owing to insufficient structural alignment with mission chains, the absence of higher-order cognitive supervision, and poor correspondence between data quality criteria and engineering specifications. The core bottleneck is the construction of high-quality supervised fine-tuning (SFT) datasets. To this end, we propose BD-FDG (Bloom's Taxonomy-based Domain-specific Fine-tuning Data Generation), a framework that addresses incomplete knowledge coverage, shallow cognitive depth, and limited quality controllability through three mechanisms: structured knowledge organization, cognitively layered question modeling, and automated quality control. The framework uses a knowledge tree to ensure structured corpus coverage, designs a question generation scheme spanning nine categories and six cognitive levels from Remember to Create to produce samples with a continuous difficulty gradient, and applies a multidimensional scoring pipeline to enforce domain rigor and consistency. Using BD-FDG, we construct SSA-SFT, a domain dataset of approximately 230K samples, and fine-tune Qwen3-8B to obtain SSA-LLM-8B. Experiments show that SSA-LLM-8B achieves relative BLEU-1 improvements of 144\% (no-think) and 176\% (think) on the domain test set and a win rate of 82.21\% over the baseline in arena comparisons, while largely preserving general benchmark performance (MMLU-Pro, MATH-500). These results validate SFT data construction driven by cognitive layering as an effective paradigm for complex engineering domains and provide a transferable framework for domain-specific LLM adaptation.

</details>


### [219] [Social-R1: Towards Human-like Social Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09249)
*Jincenzi Wu,Yuxuan Lei,Jianxun Lian,Yitian Huang,Lexin Zhou,Haotian Li,Xing Xie,Helen Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ToMBench-Hard对抗性基准和Social-R1强化学习框架，通过挑战性训练案例和轨迹级对齐提升语言模型的社会智能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在社会智能方面存在不足，主要依赖表面模式而非真正的社会推理，这限制了人机协作和AI服务人类需求的能力

Method: 1) 引入ToMBench-Hard对抗性基准，提供难以通过捷径解决的挑战性训练案例；2) 提出Social-R1强化学习框架，通过多维奖励（结构对齐、逻辑完整性、信息密度）监督整个推理过程，使模型推理与人类认知对齐

Result: 4B参数模型超越更大规模模型，在八个多样化基准测试中表现出强大的泛化能力

Conclusion: 挑战性训练案例结合轨迹级对齐是实现高效可靠社会智能的有效路径

Abstract: While large language models demonstrate remarkable capabilities across numerous domains, social intelligence - the capacity to perceive social cues, infer mental states, and generate appropriate responses - remains a critical challenge, particularly for enabling effective human-AI collaboration and developing AI that truly serves human needs. Current models often rely on superficial patterns rather than genuine social reasoning. We argue that cultivating human-like social intelligence requires training with challenging cases that resist shortcut solutions. To this end, we introduce ToMBench-Hard, an adversarial benchmark designed to provide hard training examples for social reasoning. Building on this, we propose Social-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that aligns model reasoning with human cognition through multi-dimensional rewards. Unlike outcome-based RL, Social-R1 supervises the entire reasoning process, enforcing structural alignment, logical integrity, and information density. Results show that our approach enables a 4B parameter model to surpass much larger counterparts and generalize robustly across eight diverse benchmarks. These findings demonstrate that challenging training cases with trajectory-level alignment offer a path toward efficient and reliable social intelligence.

</details>


### [220] [Logos: An evolvable reasoning engine for rational molecular design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09268)
*Haibin Wen,Zhe Zhao,Fanfu Wang,Tianyi Xu,Hao Zhang,Chao Yang,Ye Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Logos是一个紧凑的分子推理模型，通过整合多步逻辑推理与严格的化学一致性，在保持化学有效性的同时提供透明推理过程，实现了可靠且可解释的分子设计AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型在分子性质预测和候选生成方面存在局限性：要么具有物理保真度但缺乏透明推理，要么具有灵活推理但无法保证化学有效性。这种不平衡限制了AI系统在实际科学设计工作流程中的可靠性。

Method: 采用分阶段训练策略：1) 首先让模型接触连接分子描述与结构决策的显式推理示例；2) 逐步将这些推理模式与分子表示对齐；3) 在最终训练阶段，将化学规则和不变性直接纳入优化目标，引导模型产生化学有效的输出。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，Logos在结构准确性和化学有效性方面均表现出色，匹配或超越了参数规模大得多的通用语言模型。在涉及多个潜在冲突约束的分子优化任务中表现出稳定行为，并通过显式展示中间推理步骤支持人类对设计逻辑的检查和评估。

Conclusion: 联合优化推理结构和物理一致性为分子科学提供了可靠且可解释的AI系统的实用途径，支持人工智能更紧密地融入科学发现过程。

Abstract: The discovery and design of functional molecules remain central challenges across chemistry,biology, and materials science. While recent advances in machine learning have accelerated molecular property prediction and candidate generation, existing models tend to excel either in physical fidelity without transparent reasoning, or in flexible reasoning without guarantees of chemical validity. This imbalance limits the reliability of artificial intelligence systems in real scientific design workflows.Here we present Logos, a compact molecular reasoning model that integrates multi-step logical reasoning with strict chemical consistency. Logos is trained using a staged strategy that first exposes the model to explicit reasoning examples linking molecular descriptions to structural decisions, and then progressively aligns these reasoning patterns with molecular representations. In a final training phase, chemical rules and invariants are incorporated directly into the optimization objective, guiding the model toward chemically valid outputs. Across multiple benchmark datasets, Logos achieves strong performance in both structural accuracy and chemical validity, matching or surpassing substantially larger general-purpose language models while operating with a fraction of their parameters. Beyond benchmark evaluation, the model exhibits stable behaviour in molecular optimization tasks involving multiple, potentially conflicting constraints. By explicitly exposing intermediate reasoning steps, Logos enables human inspection and assessment of the design logic underlying each generated structure. These results indicate that jointly optimizing for reasoning structure and physical consistency offers a practical pathway toward reliable and interpretable AI systems for molecular science, supporting closer integration of artificial intelligence into scientific discovery processes.

</details>


### [221] [Rescaling Confidence: What Scale Design Reveals About LLM Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09309)
*Yuyang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM的口头置信度存在严重离散化问题，78%以上响应集中在三个整数上；通过系统操纵置信度尺度的三个维度，发现0-20尺度比标准0-100格式能提高元认知效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM口头置信度（报告数值确定性分数）被广泛用于黑盒设置中的不确定性估计，但置信度尺度本身（通常是0-100）很少被检验。研究者发现这种设计选择并非中立，需要系统研究尺度设计如何影响口头不确定性质量

Method: 在6个LLM和3个数据集上分析口头置信度的分布特征；系统操纵置信度尺度的三个维度：粒度、边界位置和范围规律性；使用元d'评估元认知敏感性；比较不同尺度设计对元认知效率的影响

Result: 口头置信度严重离散化，超过78%的响应集中在三个整数上；0-20尺度相比标准0-100格式能持续提高元认知效率；边界压缩会降低性能；整数偏好即使在非规律范围下仍然存在

Conclusion: 置信度尺度设计直接影响口头不确定性的质量，应被视为LLM评估中的首要实验变量；尺度选择对元认知性能有显著影响，需要更精细的尺度设计考虑

Abstract: Verbalized confidence, in which LLMs report a numerical certainty score, is widely used to estimate uncertainty in black-box settings, yet the confidence scale itself (typically 0--100) is rarely examined. We show that this design choice is not neutral. Across six LLMs and three datasets, verbalized confidence is heavily discretized, with more than 78% of responses concentrating on just three round-number values. To investigate this phenomenon, we systematically manipulate confidence scales along three dimensions: granularity, boundary placement, and range regularity, and evaluate metacognitive sensitivity using meta-d'. We find that a 0--20 scale consistently improves metacognitive efficiency over the standard 0--100 format, while boundary compression degrades performance and round-number preferences persist even under irregular ranges. These results demonstrate that confidence scale design directly affects the quality of verbalized uncertainty and should be treated as a first-class experimental variable in LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [222] [Curveball Steering: The Right Direction To Steer Isn't Always Linear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09313)
*Shivam Raval,Hae Jin Song,Linlin Wu,Abir Harrasse,Jeff Phillips,Amirali Abdullah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文质疑线性表示假说，提出激活空间存在显著几何扭曲，并基于多项式核PCA开发非线性干预方法Curveball steering，在几何扭曲强烈时优于线性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活导向方法主要依赖线性表示假说，假设行为属性可通过全局线性方向操纵。但实践中线性干预常表现不一致，作者质疑该假设并分析LLM激活空间的内在几何特性。

Method: 通过测地线与欧氏距离之比测量几何扭曲，发现激活空间存在显著概念依赖性扭曲。提出基于多项式核PCA的非线性导向方法Curveball steering，在特征空间中执行干预以更好尊重学习到的激活几何。

Result: 观察到激活空间存在实质性几何扭曲，无法用全局线性几何良好近似。Curveball steering在几何扭曲强烈的机制中一致优于基于线性PCA的导向方法。

Conclusion: 几何感知的非线性导向为全局线性干预提供了原则性替代方案，激活空间的非线性几何特性需要被考虑以改进LLM行为控制。

Abstract: Activation steering is a widely used approach for controlling large language model (LLM) behavior by intervening on internal representations. Existing methods largely rely on the Linear Representation Hypothesis, assuming behavioral attributes can be manipulated using global linear directions. In practice, however, such linear interventions often behave inconsistently. We question this assumption by analyzing the intrinsic geometry of LLM activation spaces. Measuring geometric distortion via the ratio of geodesic to Euclidean distances, we observe substantial and concept-dependent distortions, indicating that activation spaces are not well-approximated by a globally linear geometry. Motivated by this, we propose "Curveball steering", a nonlinear steering method based on polynomial kernel PCA that performs interventions in a feature space, better respecting the learned activation geometry. Curveball steering consistently outperforms linear PCA-based steering, particularly in regimes exhibiting strong geometric distortion, suggesting that geometry-aware, nonlinear steering provides a principled alternative to global, linear interventions.

</details>


### [223] [Robust Regularized Policy Iteration under Transition Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09344)
*Hongqiang Lin,Zhenghui Fu,Weihao Tang,Pengfei Wang,Yiding Sun,Qixian Huang,Dongxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RRPI提出了一种鲁棒正则化策略迭代方法，通过将离线RL建模为鲁棒策略优化问题，处理分布偏移下的外推和转移不确定性，在D4RL基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在分布偏移下性能下降，学习到的策略可能访问值估计和学习动态不可靠的分布外状态-动作对，需要解决策略诱导的外推和转移不确定性。

Method: 将离线RL建模为鲁棒策略优化，将转移核作为不确定性集内的决策变量，针对最坏情况动态优化策略。提出RRPI，用KL正则化替代不可处理的max-min双层目标，基于鲁棒正则化贝尔曼算子设计高效策略迭代过程。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中，RRPI实现了强大的平均性能，在大多数环境中优于包括PMDB在内的最新基线方法，同时在其余环境中保持竞争力。学习到的Q值在认知不确定性较高的区域下降，表明策略在转移不确定性下避免了不可靠的分布外动作。

Conclusion: RRPI通过鲁棒正则化策略迭代有效处理离线RL中的分布偏移问题，理论证明算子具有γ收缩性，迭代更新能单调改进原始鲁棒目标，实验验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables data-efficient and safe policy learning without online exploration, but its performance often degrades under distribution shift. The learned policy may visit out-of-distribution state-action pairs where value estimates and learned dynamics are unreliable. To address policy-induced extrapolation and transition uncertainty in a unified framework, we formulate offline RL as robust policy optimization, treating the transition kernel as a decision variable within an uncertainty set and optimizing the policy against the worst-case dynamics. We propose Robust Regularized Policy Iteration (RRPI), which replaces the intractable max-min bilevel objective with a tractable KL-regularized surrogate and derives an efficient policy iteration procedure based on a robust regularized Bellman operator. We provide theoretical guarantees by showing that the proposed operator is a $γ$-contraction and that iteratively updating the surrogate yields monotonic improvement of the original robust objective with convergence. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that RRPI achieves strong average performance, outperforming recent baselines including percentile-based methods such as PMDB on the majority of environments while remaining competitive on the rest. Moreover, RRPI exhibits robust behavior. The learned $Q$-values decrease in regions with higher epistemic uncertainty, suggesting that the resulting policy avoids unreliable out-of-distribution actions under transition uncertainty.

</details>


### [224] [AI Act Evaluation Benchmark: An Open, Transparent, and Reproducible Evaluation Dataset for NLP and RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09435)
*Athanasios Davvetas,Michael Papademas,Xenia Ziouvelou,Vangelis Karkaletsis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创建资源的方法，用于评估NLP模型（特别是RAG系统）对欧盟AI法案的合规性，通过生成包含风险分类、条款检索、义务生成和问答任务的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在公共和社会领域的快速部署增加了对监管标准合规性的需求，但缺乏资源阻碍了对AI系统合规性的半自动或自动评估，导致需要容易出错的人工工作。

Method: 开发了一种开放、透明、可重复的方法，利用领域知识作为解释基础，结合大语言模型的处理和推理能力，生成包含风险级别分类、条款检索、义务生成和问答任务的EU AI Act数据集。

Result: 创建了机器对机器格式的数据集，能够处理风险级别边界不明确的情况（如有限和最小风险案例），基于RAG的解决方案在禁止和高风险场景下分别达到0.87和0.85的F1分数。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了如何利用语言模型进行基于文档的高相关性生成，为评估AI系统对EU AI Act的合规性提供了有效资源，克服了监管标准中风险级别边界不明确的问题。

Abstract: The rapid rollout of AI in heterogeneous public and societal sectors has subsequently escalated the need for compliance with regulatory standards and frameworks. The EU AI Act has emerged as a landmark in the regulatory landscape. The development of solutions that elicit the level of AI systems' compliance with such standards is often limited by the lack of resources, hindering the semi-automated or automated evaluation of their performance. This generates the need for manual work, which is often error-prone, resource-limited or limited to cases not clearly described by the regulation. This paper presents an open, transparent, and reproducible method of creating a resource that facilitates the evaluation of NLP models with a strong focus on RAG systems. We have developed a dataset that contain the tasks of risk-level classification, article retrieval, obligation generation, and question-answering for the EU AI Act. The dataset files are in a machine-to-machine appropriate format. To generate the files, we utilise domain knowledge as an exegetical basis, combining with the processing and reasoning power of large language models to generate scenarios along with the respective tasks. Our methodology demonstrates a way to harness language models for grounded generation with high document relevancy. Besides, we overcome limitations such as navigating the decision boundaries of risk-levels that are not explicitly defined within the EU AI Act, such as limited and minimal cases. Finally, we demonstrate our dataset's effectiveness by evaluating a RAG-based solution that reaches 0.87 and 0.85 F1-score for prohibited and high-risk scenarios.

</details>


### [225] [An Empirical Study and Theoretical Explanation on Task-Level Model-Merging Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09463)
*Yuan Cao,Dezhi Ran,Yuzhe Guo,Mengzhou Wu,Simin Chen,Linyi Li,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现模型合并存在"合并崩溃"现象：某些任务组合在合并后会出现灾难性性能下降，这与任务间的表示不兼容性高度相关，而参数空间冲突指标相关性很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并能够统一同一基础模型上独立微调的LLM，实现并行开发成果的复用和集成而无需重新训练。但在实践中发现合并并不总是成功，某些任务专家模型的组合在合并后会出现灾难性的性能下降，这种现象被称为"合并崩溃"。研究旨在识别和表征这种任务级别的合并崩溃现象。

Method: 通过大量实验和统计分析，研究任务间表示不兼容性与合并崩溃的相关性，同时对比参数空间冲突指标的相关性。从理论角度，通过率失真理论结合维度相关界限来解释这一现象，建立任务可合并性的基本限制。

Result: 研究发现任务间的表示不兼容性与合并崩溃有强相关性，而参数空间冲突指标相关性很小，这与模型合并文献中的传统认知相悖。某些任务组合在所有合并方法中都会触发巨大的性能下降。

Conclusion: 任务级别的合并崩溃现象确实存在，其根本原因在于任务间的表示不兼容性，而非参数空间冲突。通过率失真理论建立了任务可合并性的基本限制，表明某些任务组合无论采用何种合并方法都无法成功合并。

Abstract: Model merging unifies independently fine-tuned LLMs from the same base, enabling reuse and integration of parallel development efforts without retraining. However, in practice we observe that merging does not always succeed: certain combinations of task-specialist models suffer from catastrophic performance degradation after merging. We refer to this failure mode as merging collapse. Intuitively, collapse arises when the learned representations or parameter adjustments for different tasks are fundamentally incompatible, so that merging forces destructive interference rather than synergy. In this paper, we identify and characterize the phenomenon of task-level merging collapse, where certain task combinations consistently trigger huge performance degradation across all merging methods. Through extensive experiments and statistical analysis, we demonstrate that representational incompatibility between tasks is strongly correlated with merging collapse, while parameter-space conflict metrics show minimal correlation, challenging conventional wisdom in model merging literature. We provide a theoretical explanation on this phenomenon through rate-distortion theory with a dimension-dependent bound, establishing fundamental limits on task mergeability regardless of methodology.

</details>


### [226] [Telogenesis: Goal Is All U Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09476)
*Zhuoran Deng,Yizhi Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Wan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于认知缺口（无知、惊讶、陈旧）生成注意优先级的函数，验证了在没有外部奖励的情况下，仅凭内部认知状态就能产生自适应注意力分配策略，并能自发恢复环境波动结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
